In controlled full-batch teacher--student dynamics, we find that the first-step spike--bulk gap alone does not explain onset time. Instead, finite-onset regression supports a dimension-corrected spectral-gap law, (τ_{\mathrm{HT}}\approx CΔ_1^{-γ}d^ρ), with (R^2=0.683), (γ=0.626), and (ρ=0.772) across 330 completed runs. Right-censored lognormal accelerated-failure-time models further favor the dimension-corrected model over a gap-only model, improving AIC from 706.62 to 628.70. Theoretically, we prove that exact early loss dynamics in linear networks do not determine factor spectral tails, that Adam recurrences alone do not imply spectral redistribution, and that projected singular-basis spreading implies contraction of a spectral-tail potential and hence a dimension-corrected hitting-time bound. Empirically, projected-kernel profiles support the sufficient spreading mechanism, Adam and AdamW agree under tested grids, GD and signGD do not reach onset in the same regimes, and real pretrained Qwen2.5-0.5B and Pythia-70M transformer weights show non-Gaussian spectral-tail structure relative to matched Gaussian nulls. The result is a reproducible spectral hitting-time law with rigorous conditional theory, not a claim that Adam necessarily generates heavy tails from first principles.

</details>


### [197] [A Full Adam Theorem for Spectral Heavy-Tail Onset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12996)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在闭合的高斯斯坦-埃尔米特教师-学生状态演化模型中，谱重尾启动的完整Adam定理。从全批量Adam递推出发，通过斯坦-埃尔米特微积分推导出种群梯度，证明有限宽度协方差集中性，将多步Adam动量转化为精确的非中心高斯符号核，通过基均质化定理控制对角Adam分母，从埃尔米特边缘转移定理导出规则变化的投影更新响应，将响应推进到精确格拉姆更新，并证明具有匹配上下界击中时间的近似目标KL收缩。最终的规律为 (τ_\varepsilon=Θ(Δ_1^{-γ}d^ρ\log(Ψ_0/\varepsilon)))，其中 (Δ_1) 是第一个峰-体谱间隙。结果是完全的：从Adam动量和分母到谱击中定律的每一步都在封闭的状态演化模型中形式化。此外，还证明了更强的任意梯度Adam定理是不可能的，且精确两步线性网络损失动态无法识别因子谱或重尾击中时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Adam优化器在重尾谱分布下的收敛行为，特别是在高斯斯坦-埃尔米特教师-学生模型中的表现，以揭示其在复杂数据结构下的理论极限与机制。

Method: 采用斯坦-埃尔米特微积分推导种群梯度，结合有限宽度协方差集中性、基均质化定理、赫尔米特边缘转移定理等工具，将多步动量转化为非中心高斯符号核，构建精确格拉姆更新，并分析KL散度收缩与击中时间边界。

Result: (τ_\\varepsilon=Θ(Δ_1^{-γ}d^ρ\\log(Ψ_0/\\varepsilon)))，即击中时间与谱间隙、维度、初始误差比相关；同时证明强于该结果的任意梯度定理不可能成立，且两步线性网络无法捕捉因子谱或重尾击中时间。

Conclusion: 本文建立了重尾谱环境下Adam优化器的完整理论框架，揭示了其收敛速率的精确依赖关系，并表明现有简化模型无法捕捉关键动力学特征，为理解深度学习优化提供了深刻洞察。

Abstract: We prove a full Adam theorem for spectral heavy-tail onset in a closed Gaussian Stein-Hermite teacher-student state-evolution model. The theorem begins with the actual full-batch Adam recurrences, derives the population gradient by Stein-Hermite calculus, proves finite-width covariance concentration, converts multi-step Adam momentum into an exact non-centered Gaussian sign kernel, controls the diagonal Adam denominator by a basis-homogenization theorem, derives a regularly varying projected update response from a Hermite edge-transfer theorem, pushes the response through the exact Gram update, and proves approximate-target KL contraction with matching upper and lower hitting bounds. The final law is (τ_\varepsilon=Θ(Δ_1^{-γ}d^ρ\log(Ψ_0/\varepsilon))), where (Δ_1) is the first spike-bulk spectral gap. The result is full in the following precise sense: every step from Adam's momentum and denominator to the spectral hitting law is formalized inside the closed state-evolution model. We also prove that a stronger arbitrary-gradient Adam theorem is impossible, and that exact two-step linear-network loss dynamics do not identify factor spectra or heavy-tail hitting times.

</details>


### [198] [Dual-guided Hierarchical Edge Localization for Large-scale Optimal Transport Across Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13010)
*Wenzhou Xia,Qiaoqiao Ding,Jingwei Liang,Xiaoqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HELLO 是一种层次化求解器，用于大规模离散最优传输（OT）问题。它通过双对偶势能引导粗粒度到细粒度的初始化和层级内优化，实现高效的边缘定位与收敛。利用预算剪枝策略保持线性内存复杂度，并在精确算术下证明了在符号字典序规则下的有限终止性。在百万级样本规模下，相比强基线方法，HELLO 在多种特征维度上实现了更低的传输目标和数量级的加速。其可扩展至每边际128万样本、8192维，在单个H100 GPU上仅使用41.6 GiB峰值显存，满足相对KKT残差低于$10^{-6}$。该框架还可推广至一般成对代价及半离散OT、Gromov–Wasserstein、非平衡OT和基于OT的流匹配等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散最优传输（OT）在无正则化情况下需求解具有二次量级运输变量的线性规划，计算成本高，难以处理大规模数据。现有方法在精度、效率或可扩展性方面存在局限，亟需一种高效且可扩展的求解框架以支持现代机器学习中的大规模分布对齐需求。

Method: 提出HELLO框架，采用递归子采样层次结构进行粗到细初始化，利用双对偶势能引导候选边分配；通过迭代插入最大对偶违反项进行层级内优化，并结合预算剪枝控制内存开销；采用符号字典序规则保证精确算术下有限终止于全局最优解。

Result: 在百万点规模下，相较于强基线，HELLO在不同特征维度上均实现显著更低的运输目标与数量级更快的运行速度；成功处理每边际128万样本、8192维的数据，在单张H100 GPU上仅用41.6 GiB显存，相对KKT残差低于$10^{-6}$；可广泛应用于一般成对代价、半离散OT、Gromov–Wasserstein、非平衡OT及流匹配等多种场景。

Conclusion: HELLO提供了一种高效、可扩展且理论上保障收敛的最优传输求解新范式，适用于多种复杂场景，为大规模分布对齐任务提供了强有力的工具支持。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) compares distributions and aligns datasets in machine learning, yet unregularized discrete OT requires a linear program with quadratically many transport variables. We propose HELLO, a hierarchical solver that casts large-scale discrete OT as edge localization and uses dual potentials to guide both coarse-to-fine initialization and within-level refinement. Initialization propagates coarse dual potentials across a recursive subsampling hierarchy to assign candidate edges. Refinement then iteratively inserts the largest dual violators in each row and column until the relative KKT residual meets a prescribed tolerance, while budgeted pruning ensures linear memory complexity. For exact-arithmetic refinement, we prove finite termination at a global optimum under a symbolic lexicographic rule. At the million-point scale, HELLO attains lower transport objectives with order-of-magnitude runtime improvements over strong baselines across feature dimensions from single digits to thousands. It further scales to 1.28 million samples per marginal in 8192 dimensions on a single H100, using 41.6 GiB peak GPU memory while satisfying a full relative KKT residual below $10^{-6}$. Beyond standard discrete OT, the framework supports general pairwise costs and serves as a scalable balanced-OT oracle for semi-discrete OT, Gromov--Wasserstein, unbalanced OT, and OT-based Flow Matching.

</details>


### [199] [Attention Quantization for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13031)
*Jonas M. Kübler,Benjamin Jäger,Klemens Flöge,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对表格基础模型的推理效率问题，提出一种基于FP8量化的新策略，重点优化注意力计算。通过显式使用FP8矩阵乘法指令，并确保训练与测试阶段量化误差对齐，实现最高1.7倍的速度提升，且在TabPFN-v3和TabICLv2上保持准确率无显著损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型虽与大语言模型架构相似，但规模和部署模式不同，现有针对大语言模型的量化方法（如权重或KV缓存量化）不适用，需聚焦于注意力计算的优化以提升推理效率。

Method: 提出对查询、键、值进行FP8量化，利用显式FP8矩阵乘法指令加速注意力计算，并设计机制确保训练与测试阶段量化误差一致。

Result: 在Triton内核上实现最高1.7倍于16位常规内核的加速，且在TabArena和BeyondArena基准测试中未出现明显准确率下降。

Conclusion: 对于表格基础模型，注意力计算的FP8量化是高效推理的关键，通过误差对齐可实现高性能与高精度的平衡。

Abstract: With the recent rise and adoption of tabular foundation models, optimizing their inference performance becomes an emerging field for efficiency research. While the models are architecturally similar to transformer-based large language models (LLMs), the size and serving patterns differ significantly. We show that the focus should be on the attention calculation and less on weight or KV cache quantization, which are more popular in LLMs. We develop a quantization strategy for queries, keys, and values to FP8 and use explicit FP8 matrix multiplication instructions to speed up the attention calculation. We find that it is crucial to align the quantization error in the test rows with the quantization error in the training rows, as otherwise the accuracy drops drastically. Our Triton kernel achieves a speedup up to 1.7x over regular 16-bit kernels, and we show that on TabPFN-v3 and TabICLv2 there is no relevant accuracy loss across TabArena and BeyondArena.

</details>


### [200] [Groupoid-Based Internal State Representations for Reinforcement Learning with Local Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13035)
*Ben Opperman,Eduardo Alonso,Esther Mondragón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于群胚（groupoid）的强化学习框架，用于捕捉状态相关的局部对称性，并在交互过程中动态发现等价结构。该方法通过维护轨道代表元和传输器，将原始状态映射到规范形式，使学习与决策在对称性约简空间中进行，同时保留局部差异。实验表明，该方法在具有强部分对称性的密集和大规模环境中显著提升了样本效率与收敛速度，性能优于标准Q-learning。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖固定的群作用或预定义的状态抽象，难以利用现实环境中存在的模块化和局部上下文相关规律。为克服这一限制，本文旨在通过动态发现局部对称性来提升算法的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出基于群胚的强化学习框架，利用轨道代表元和传输器实现状态的规范表示，支持在对称性约简空间中进行学习与决策，同时保持局部区分性。

Result: 在具有强部分对称性的密集和大规模环境中，该方法显著提升了样本效率和收敛速度，相比标准Q-learning获得明显性能提升。

Conclusion: 动态利用局部对称性为可扩展、可泛化的强化学习提供了一条数学上严谨且实用的路径。

Abstract: Symmetries play a central role in reducing the complexity of reinforcement learning problems, yet most existing approaches rely on fixed group actions or predefined state abstractions. Classical reinforcement learning algorithms typically assume a globally structured Markov decision process with uniformly applicable actions and transitions, an assumption that limits their ability to exploit modularity and local, context-dependent regularities present in many realistic environments. We propose a reinforcement learning framework using groupoids to capture local, state-dependent symmetries and support the dy- namic discovery of equivalence structures during interaction. The agent maintains orbit representatives together with transporters that map raw states to canonical forms, enabling learning and decision-making to be performed in a symmetry-reduced space while preserving local distinctions. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed groupoid-based approach improves sample efficiency and convergence in dense and large-scale environments exhibiting strong partial symmetries, yielding substantial performance gains over standard Q-learning. These findings show that dynamically exploiting local symmetry provides a practical and mathematically principled route to scalable and generalisable reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [201] [Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13039)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Jacopo Bono,Hugo Ferreira,Pedro Ribeiro,Pedro Saleiro,Pedro Bizarro,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的迁移学习框架——针对动态演化的领域（TrED），旨在解决现实世界中数据随时间逐步积累的场景。传统迁移学习被划分为多个孤立的子领域（如域泛化、域适应等），但这些设定仅描述了系统在部署过程中某一时刻的快照。TrED将整个数据演化过程建模为一个统一的学习问题，包含数据可用性过程、学习协议和整体轨迹评估标准。该框架将经典设置视为系统在不同阶段经历的过渡状态，而非独立问题。通过对现有文献的分析，发现大多数方法仅针对单一场景设计，且缺乏对整个演化轨迹的优化能力。因此，作者认为TrED是一个合理且尚未解决的重要研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实应用场景中，数据和标签是逐步积累的，而非一次性提供，传统的迁移学习设定（如域适应、域泛化）仅反映某一固定状态，无法捕捉数据随时间演化的动态过程。因此需要一种新的框架来统一处理这一连续变化的数据可用性问题。

Method: 提出并形式化一个名为Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains (TrED)的新框架，定义其核心要素：由环境决定的数据可用性过程、学习者可选择的学习协议、以及基于整个模型演化轨迹的评估标准。通过此框架，将经典迁移学习设置重新理解为系统在演化路径中的不同阶段。

Result: 分析表明，当前大多数迁移学习方法都针对特定静态场景设计，未能有效优化整个数据演化过程中的性能轨迹。即使最强的方法也未实现全轨迹最优，说明现有工作存在明显缺口。

Conclusion: TrED是一个具有明确形式定义且尚未解决的关键挑战，代表了未来迁移学习研究的重要方向。

Abstract: Transfer learning explores how to leverage knowledge from various tasks or domains (sources) to enhance predictive performance in related tasks or domains (targets). Typically, transfer learning research is segmented into several isolated sub-areas (such as domain generalisation, domain adaptation, or multi-domain learning), each making distinct assumptions about target data availability, namely how much data and how many labels are available at training time. However, in many real-world applications, data availability is not fixed but evolves over time, as instances and labels are progressively collected from a new domain. Each of the classical settings then describes only a snapshot of a trajectory that a deployed system must traverse in full. We formalise this trajectory as a transfer learning problem in its own right, Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains (TrED), specified by a data availability process fixed by the environment, a learning protocol that the method is free to choose, and an evaluation criterion that scores the whole trajectory of models rather than a single one. Within this formalism, the classical settings are recovered as regimes that a learner may pass through, rather than as separate problems that TrED concatenates. We then examine the transfer learning literature to identify mechanisms that are promising building blocks for a solution, and find that most methods are tailored to a single regime and that even the strongest existing candidates do not yet optimise the whole trajectory. We argue that TrED is a well-posed and unsolved problem, and an important direction for future research.

</details>


### [202] [Quantile-based Loss Filtering for Outlier-Robust Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13040)
*Jamie Haddock,Anna Ma,Elizaveta Rebrova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的损失过滤框架——量纲-k损失随机梯度下降（QkL-SGD），通过在每次迭代中采样k个组件损失，并从下部经验q分位数中均匀选择一个索引进行更新，以应对有限求和优化中部分组件函数被污染、梯度不可靠的问题。该方法在标准凸性假设下证明了线性收敛性，要求样本大小与污染数量及子集强凸阈值相关。对于无法或不希望采用大样本的情况，给出了互补的小样本概率分析，表明收敛行为取决于选中异常值的概率以及所选良步的曲率。实验结果表明，中间分位数通常优于标准SGD和min-k-loss SGD；后者常因反复选择已基本解决的组件而停滞，而中间分位数保持鲁棒性并产生更富信息量的更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有限求和优化中部分组件函数被污染、其梯度可能高度不可靠的问题，现有方法如min-k-loss SGD和分位数法在处理此类问题时存在局限性，尤其是在样本量受限或频繁选择已解决组件导致停滞的情况下。因此，需要一种更具鲁棒性和适应性的损失过滤机制。

Method: 提出量纲-k损失随机梯度下降（QkL-SGD）框架：每轮迭代采样k个组件损失，从中均匀选取位于下部经验q分位数中的索引进行参数更新。该方法结合了最小损失与分位数思想，既避免极端异常值影响，又防止过度聚焦于已接近收敛的组件。理论分析包括大样本下的线性收敛性证明（依赖样本规模与污染程度）以及小样本情形下的概率收敛分析。

Result: 在标准凸性假设下，QkL-SGD实现线性收敛，所需样本规模与污染数量和子集强凸性阈值成正比。小样本情况下，收敛性能依赖于选中异常值的概率和良步的曲率。实验验证了中间分位数策略在多项式回归、正则化逻辑回归和正则化铰链损失任务中的优越性：相比标准SGD和min-k-loss SGD，中间分位数能更好避免停滞，提升更新质量与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: QkL-SGD提供了一种灵活且高效的损失过滤机制，在不同样本规模下均表现出良好性能。中间分位数策略在实际应用中往往优于极端的min-k-loss方法，因其兼具鲁棒性与信息丰富性，是处理带污染优化问题的有效方案。

Abstract: We study loss-based filtering for finite-sum optimization with a subset of corrupted component functions whose gradients may be highly unreliable. Motivated by minimum-loss-based SGD (min-$k$-loss) and quantile-based methods for corrupted linear systems, we propose and analyze a general loss-filtering framework -- Quantile-\(k\)-Loss SGD (Q\(k\)L-SGD) -- that samples \(k\) component losses at each iteration and updates using an index chosen uniformly from the lower empirical \(q\)-quantile. We prove linear convergence of this family of methods under standard convexity assumptions, requiring the sample size to scale with the number of corruptions and a subset strong-convexity threshold. For the cases when large enough sampling is impossible or undesirable, we give a complementary small-sample probabilistic analysis that covers any sample size $k$ and the convergence behavior depends on the probability of selecting an outlier and on the curvature of the selected good step. Experiments on polynomial regression, regularized logistic regression, and regularized hinge loss show that intermediate quantiles often outperform both standard SGD and min-\(k\)-loss SGD. In particular, min-\(k\) often stalls by repeatedly selecting nearly solved components, while intermediate quantiles retain robustness and produce more informative updates.

</details>


### [203] [DynSHAP: Towards Explainable Dynamic Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13042)
*Nastasya Anokhina,Jonas Jürß,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DynSHAP和Temporal DynSHAP框架，解决动态生存分析中纵向、不规则输入与功能型生存输出的可解释性难题。通过将时间-特征对视为Shapley博弈中的参与者，实现对模型预测的精准归因，在合成数据和真实临床数据上均表现出优越的可解释性，提升医疗专家对模型的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法无法同时处理动态生存分析中的纵向、不规则输入和功能型生存输出，限制了深度学习模型在临床场景中的信任与应用。

Method: 提出DynSHAP框架，将时间-特征对作为Shapley博弈中的玩家；引入Temporal DynSHAP，通过学习特征随时间的线性依赖关系并采用条件采样进行解释，增强对时序依赖特征的归因能力。

Result: 在合成数据上，Temporal DynSHAP比传统边际估计器更准确地恢复时序依赖特征；在两个真实临床数据集和两种DSA架构上，生成的归因结果忠实反映模型学习机制，帮助医生理解预测依据及其发生时间。

Conclusion: DynSHAP和Temporal DynSHAP有效提升了动态生存分析模型的可解释性，为临床部署提供了可信的解释支持，推动深度学习在医疗决策中的实际应用。

Abstract: Deep learning models for dynamic survival analysis (DSA) achieve strong predictive performance by incorporating longitudinal patient data, but their black box nature limits clinical trust and adoption. Existing explainability methods cannot handle longitudinal, irregular inputs and functional survival outputs simultaneously, which limits their usability in DSA. We propose DynSHAP, a SHAP framework suited specifically for dynamic survival analysis. It extends common marginal SHAP estimators to this setting by treating time--feature pairs as players in the Shapley game. We further introduce Temporal DynSHAP, which learns linear dependencies in features over time and uses conditional sampling to address them in explanations. When applied to synthetic data with known ground-truth attributions, Temporal DynSHAP recovers temporally dependent features more accurately than marginal estimators for a given state-of-the-art model. Applied to two real-world clinical datasets and two DSA architectures, DynSHAP produces attributions faithful to model learning, allowing medical experts to see which patient information drove the prediction and when.

</details>


### [204] [Robust Policy Optimization via Adversarial Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13044)
*Amine Andam,Jamal Bentahar,Mustapha Hedabou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对深度强化学习（DRL）在输入扰动下的鲁棒性问题，从算法设计、实现和评估三个阶段提出改进。提出Adversarial Importance Sampling（Advis）方法，通过标准训练轨迹的重要性采样估计并优化可验证的最坏情况回报，满足无需额外环境交互、无需辅助网络、捕捉长期鲁棒性等三个优势；开发了名为advrl的模块化PyTorch库，支持快速原型设计与可复现评估；指出基于学习的攻击者在不同智能体间超参数不具迁移性，因此采用更广泛多样的攻击配置（6-14倍于以往）进行评估，并在连续控制环境中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL鲁棒性方法在算法设计、实现和评估阶段存在局限：缺乏高效且无需额外训练的鲁棒性评估手段，实现方式复杂难复现，评估中攻击配置不足导致对鲁棒性的高估。

Method: 提出Advis方法，利用重要性采样在标准训练轨迹上估计最坏情况回报；构建advrl库，提供简洁、模块化的鲁棒性方法与攻击实现；采用大规模多样化攻击配置进行评估，避免因有限攻击集导致的性能误判。

Result: Advis在无需额外环境交互和辅助网络的前提下实现了长期鲁棒性优化；advrl提升了方法实现的可读性与可复现性；大规模攻击评估揭示了超参数不可迁移性，并证明所提方法在连续控制任务中优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 本工作系统性地推进了DRL鲁棒性研究，通过新方法、新工具和更严格的评估范式，显著提升了鲁棒策略的设计与验证质量。

Abstract: Significant progress has been made in safeguarding deep reinforcement learning (DRL) policies against input perturbations. Developing robust DRL involves three main stages: algorithm design, implementation, and evaluation. In this work, we identify and address a key limitation at each stage. First, we introduce Adversarial Importance Sampling (Advis), a method that uses importance sampling over trajectories from standard training to estimate and optimize verifiable worst-case returns. Advis satisfies three desirable criteria not jointly achieved by prior work: it requires no additional environment interactions, no auxiliary networks, and captures long-term robustness. Second, we introduce advrl, a modular PyTorch library that provides clean, single-file implementations of existing robustness methods and adversarial attacks, facilitating rapid prototyping and enabling reproducible and traceable evaluations. Third, we revisit evaluation under learned adversaries and show that optimal adversarial hyperparameters do not transfer across agents, which can lead to an overestimation of robustness when using a limited set of attacker configurations. Accordingly, we evaluate policies against a large and diverse set of attackers, using 6-14x more configurations than prior work. Finally, we evaluate our approach on continuous control environments, demonstrating its effectiveness relative to existing baselines. The code is available at: https://github.com/AmineAndam04/advrl

</details>


### [205] [MCRL2: Multi-resource Cross-attention-based Representation Learning-augmented Reinforcement Learning for Cloud Microservice Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13048)
*Tiangang Li,Shi Ying,Xiangbo Tian,Chuan Shi,Ding Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCRL2是一种基于多资源交叉注意力的表示学习增强型强化学习方法，用于解决微服务调度中的负载均衡和资源异构性挑战。通过引入跨资源注意力机制，有效捕捉节点、资源与微服务间的复杂依赖关系，并结合最大熵目标提升策略稳定性，显著优于现有基线方法，在真实集群数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在微服务调度中难以捕捉异构资源间的复杂耦合关系，且忽略系统状态的表征学习重要性，导致负载不均和调度效率低下。

Method: 提出MCRL，利用多资源交叉注意力机制建模节点、资源与微服务之间的结构化交互；在此基础上构建MCRL2，采用增强型演员-评论家架构与最大熵目标，提升状态表达能力与决策性能。

Result: 在真实生产集群轨迹上的实验表明，MCRL2在负载均衡、调度成功率和平均完成时间等指标上均显著优于现有方法，适用于多种工作负载模式。

Conclusion: MCRL2通过融合多资源交叉注意力的表示学习与强化学习，有效提升了微服务调度的智能性与鲁棒性，为数据中心资源管理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient microservice scheduling is crucial for maintaining load balance across nodes in data centers and ensuring high quality of service. However, achieving this in practice remains challenging due to dynamic resource imbalance under fluctuating workloads, nonlinear coupling across multiple resource dimensions, and the heterogeneity of microservice resource demands. While reinforcement learning-based approaches have shown promise, they struggle to capture the complex interdependencies among heterogeneous resources and neglect the importance of learning informative system representations. To address these limitations, we propose MCRL2, a novel reinforcement learning approach augmented with multi-resource cross-attention-based representation learning for microservice scheduling. Specifically, we first propose MCRL, a novel representation learning approach that captures structured and informative interactions among nodes, resources, and microservices via a multi-resource cross-attention mechanism. Then, MCRL2 augments reinforcement learning through MCRL-enhanced actor-critic architecture combined with a maximum entropy objective, improving system state expressiveness and leading to more stable and effective scheduling decisions. Extensive experiments on real production cluster traces demonstrate that MCRL2 significantly outperforms existing baselines in load balancing, scheduling success rate and average completion time across diverse workload patterns.

</details>


### [206] [A Unified and Constrained View of Regularization-Based Robust Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13050)
*Amine Andam,Jamal Bentahar,Mustapha Hedabou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了基于正则化的深度强化学习鲁棒训练方法，通过推导名义策略与最坏情况策略之间的性能差距上界，揭示了添加KL惩罚项为何能提升鲁棒性。基于这些上界，将鲁棒训练建模为带约束的优化问题，并提出动态更新拉格朗日乘子的方法以自适应调节正则化权重。实验在多个连续控制任务中验证了理论分析的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则化方法在对抗输入扰动下的鲁棒性训练中虽有效，但缺乏统一的理论解释，且正则化权重通常固定，难以自适应调整。

Method: 推导性能差距的上界，将其表示为已有正则化目标加上一个策略间的KL散度惩罚项；将鲁棒训练形式化为带约束的优化问题，并采用动态更新拉格朗日乘子的方法以自适应调节正则化强度。

Result: 理论分析表明KL惩罚有助于提升鲁棒性；实验结果验证了所提方法在多个连续控制任务中优于固定正则化权重的基线方法，表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究统一了多种正则化方法，揭示了KL惩罚的作用机制，并提出一种自适应调节正则化权重的新训练范式，显著提升了深度强化学习在对抗扰动下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Regularization-based methods have become a standard approach for training Deep Reinforcement Learning policies against adversarial input perturbations. In this paper, we unify these methods by deriving new upper bounds on the performance gap between the nominal and worst-case policies. Each upper bound is expressed as an existing regularization objective plus a KL-divergence penalty between the nominal and worst-case policies, which further explains why adding a KL penalty improves robustness in practice. Building on these bounds, we formulate robust training as a constrained optimization problem, showing that existing methods correspond to the special case of a fixed Lagrange multiplier. We instead update the multiplier jointly with the policy to automatically tune the regularization weight. Finally, we conduct extensive adversarial evaluations across several continuous control tasks to validate our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [207] [Benign Loss Landscapes Can Coexist with Worst-Case Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13057)
*Zach Furman,Stephan Wäldchen,Yangda Bei,Liam Hodgkinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究树张量网络（TTNs），发现其能包含多项式大小的难学习目标，但其损失曲面在可实现目标下具有条件良性：所有最小范数的局部极小值都是全局极小值。因此，学习困难并非源于坏的局部极小值，而是由高阶退化鞍点引起，这些鞍点源于秩不足。通过奇偶函数案例分析，揭示了景观几何与计算难度之间的潜在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究真实世界任务中深度神经网络为何能有效学习，尽管存在理论上难以学习的目标；现有模型无法同时具备难学习目标和高效评估能力，因此需要新模型框架来研究此问题。

Method: 采用树张量网络（TTNs）作为模型类，证明其可嵌入任意一次读取布尔公式，并分析其损失景观结构，特别关注局部极小值与全局极小值的关系，以及鞍点的产生机制。

Result: TTNs 的损失景观对可实现目标具有条件良性，即最小范数的局部极小值必为全局极小值；学习困难主要来自高阶退化鞍点，其根源是秩不足；奇偶函数案例验证了景观几何与计算复杂性之间的关联。

Conclusion: 在树张量网络中，学习困难并非由坏的局部极小值导致，而是源于秩不足引发的高阶退化鞍点。这表明，理解损失景观的几何结构有助于揭示实际学习中的计算难度来源。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are expressive enough to contain worst-case targets that can be evaluated in polynomial time but cannot be learned in polynomial time by gradient descent. For practical tasks they nonetheless learn well, raising the question of what non-generic structure of real-world targets enables this. Existing surrogate models cannot pose this question because they either lack hard-to-learn targets entirely (deep linear networks) or cannot evaluate such targets efficiently (kernel methods, infinite-width limits). We study tree tensor networks (TTNs), a model class that generalizes deep linear networks and Tucker decompositions. We show they embed arbitrary read-once Boolean formulas, and thus contain polynomial-size targets that cannot be learned by gradient descent in polynomial time under the same mechanism as neural networks. Despite this, we prove that their loss landscapes are conditionally benign for every realizable target: every local minimum that is minimum-norm is global. Thus, surprisingly, bad local minima are not what distinguishes between typical and worst-case problems in TTNs. Instead, learning difficulty in TTNs can arise from high-order degenerate saddle points, which we show are caused by rank-deficiency. This is explored through a case study of the parity function, illustrating the potential for TTNs to relate landscape geometry to computational hardness.

</details>


### [208] [CanvasAnneal: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13060)
*Blake Olson,Yuhang Song,Emmett McQuinn,Yuan Shangguan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CanvasAnneal提出一种基于课程学习的扩散强化学习框架，通过引入强教师模型的推理先验来指导探索，在训练初期注入教师生成的推理轨迹以暖启动探索，随后逐步减少引导，促使模型独立生成推理路径。在数学推理和工具使用基准测试中，该方法优于标准diffu-GRPO，显著加速奖励提升，尤其在复杂任务上表现更优，表明结构化训练指导可有效缓解扩散强化学习中的探索瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) 虽具备并行生成能力，但在复杂推理和工具使用任务上仍落后于自回归模型；现有强化学习方法存在探索瓶颈，亟需有效引导机制以提升探索效率。

Method: 提出CanvasAnneal框架，采用课程学习策略：初始阶段将教师模型生成的推理轨迹注入扩散画布以暖启动探索，随训练进程逐步减少引导，使模型自主生成推理过程。

Result: 在MATH500、Countdown、Tau2等基准上，CanvasAnneal优于标准diffu-GRPO，显著加速奖励提升，尤其在复杂任务中优势明显，验证了结构化引导对缓解探索瓶颈的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化训练期引导能有效缓解扩散强化学习中的探索困难，加快复杂任务的收敛速度，为提升DLMs的推理与工具使用能力提供了新路径。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer promising parallel generation capabilities but lag behind autoregressive models in complex reasoning and tool-use tasks. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) has recently been applied to enhance DLMs, standard RL approaches suffer from an exploration bottleneck. To address this, we inject reasoning priors from a stronger teacher model to guide RL exploration. In this paper, we introduce CanvasAnneal, a curriculum-guided diffusion RL framework. During the initial RL phase, we warm-start exploration by injecting teacher-generated reasoning traces into the initial diffusion canvas. As training progresses, we gradually remove this guidance and require the model to generate more of the reasoning trajectory independently. Across mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, CanvasAnneal improves over standard diffu-GRPO on MATH500, Countdown, and Tau2 and substantially accelerates reward improvement on several tasks, while gains are task-dependent. Our results suggest that structured training-time guidance can alleviate exploration bottlenecks in diffusion RL and speed up convergence on harder tasks.

</details>


### [209] [RunningTensor: Generalizing Linear Attention to Higher-Order Recurrent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12814)
*Luca Herranz-Celotti,Vincent Guigue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RunningTensor，将线性注意力和状态空间模型中的递归记忆从矩阵（二阶张量）推广到高阶张量，以增强序列建模中状态的交互能力。通过秩-1外积更新并用o-1个向量查询进行读取，保持线性时间复杂度。以三阶为例，显著提升工作内存容量，优于基线方法，在合成与真实任务中均表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力和状态空间模型的递归记忆为二阶张量（矩阵），限制了状态间高阶交互能力；为提升建模能力和记忆容量，需要引入更高阶的张量结构。

Method: 提出RunningTensor，将状态表示为高阶张量（如三阶），通过秩-1外积方式更新，读取时通过与o-1个向量查询收缩实现，支持递归与并行两种形式，保持线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在合成多查询关联回忆任务中，RunningTensor优于线性注意力和状态空间模型基线；预训练后在语言理解与非合成检索任务上也取得更好性能，验证了高阶状态对记忆容量的有效提升。

Conclusion: 高阶递归状态（如三阶张量）可有效扩展模型的记忆容量，且在保持线性复杂度的前提下提升序列建模能力，为高效长序列建模提供新思路。

Abstract: Linear attention and state-space models provide linear-time sequence modeling, but their recurrent memory remains a second-order tensor (a matrix), limiting the order of interactions that can be represented in the state. We introduce the RunningTensor, which generalizes this memory to an order-$o$ tensor, updated by a rank-1 outer product and read by contracting against $o-1$ vector queries. Order $2$ recovers linear attention; we study order $3$ as a proof of concept, retaining both recurrent and parallel forms while remaining linear in sequence length $T$ and improving working memory capacity from $\mathcal{O}(W^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(W^o)$. On synthetic multi-query associative recall, RunningTensor outperforms linear-attention and SSM baselines. After pretraining, it also improves performance on language-understanding and non-synthetic retrieval tasks, suggesting that higher-order recurrent state can provide useful additional memory capacity beyond matrix-valued state.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Occamy-1.0 是基于 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 模型通过阶段式后训练得到的高效协同工作模型，专注于提升多步骤任务中的执行效率与成本效益。通过构建执行导向的数据与环境，捕捉可复现的长时程轨迹，并强化状态跟踪、协调与恢复能力，该模型在多个协同工作基准测试中表现优异，尤其在成本-性能权衡上达到低代价最优区域。其在工具调用、编程和指令遵循等任务中也保持了广泛的智能体能力，且已开源模型权重与部分训练数据以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同工作代理在复杂任务中面临高成本与延迟累积问题，尽管峰值推理能力重要，但实际应用更依赖于执行效率与稳定性。当前模型往往忽视状态管理、协调与容错等关键日常任务需求，因此需要一种兼顾高效执行与广泛能力的实用型协同模型。

Method: 采用阶段式后训练策略，构建执行接地的数据集与多场景评估环境，捕获跨多种工具链的可复现长时程任务轨迹，并通过分阶段训练逐步发展并固化信息收集、工具使用、代码生成与文件操作等互补能力。

Result: Occamy-1.0 在多个协同工作基准测试中表现领先，尤其在成本-性能权衡上位于低代价“拐点”位置；在工具调用、编程和指令遵循任务中仍保持强泛化能力，优于多数同规模模型，且与更大规模前沿系统竞争相当。

Conclusion: Occamy-1.0 证明了通过针对性后训练可有效提升协同工作模型的实际可用性，在保证广泛智能体能力的同时显著优化执行效率与成本表现，为构建实用型智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [211] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DU-NO 是一种新型的多尺度 U 形谱神经算子，通过在浅层编码器和解码器级别添加轻量级卷积 U-Net 分支，在保持高精度的同时将参数量降低至 364 万，远低于 U-FNO 的数千万参数。它在近岸波浪模拟、2D Navier-Stokes 和 PDEBench 浅水方程中均表现优异，优于现有模型，且在高频波数带仍具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高精度波浪模型（如 FUNWAVE-TVD）计算成本过高，难以用于大规模集成、不确定性量化和实时预警；而现有的神经算子虽速度快，但参数量大、效率低，亟需一种兼具高精度与低计算成本的新架构。

Method: 提出 DU-NO：一种双 U 形结构的多尺度谱神经算子，仅在最浅层的编码器与解码器引入轻量卷积分支，利用高频内容仅存在于细网格的特性，实现局部感知与谱运算的合理分工，并采用深度衰减模式调度控制参数规模。

Result: 在公开的 FUNWAVE-TVD 基准测试中，DU-NO 在自回归滚动预测中误差最低，相比 U-FNO 提升 14.9%，参数减少 10.8 倍；频率分析显示其在全频段均更优，尤其在高频段表现突出；在相同参数预算下，其他基线仍落后于 DU-NO 28.6%。此外，该模型在 2D Navier-Stokes 和浅水方程任务中也表现卓越。

Conclusion: DU-NO 实现了神经算子在波浪模拟等复杂物理系统中的高效与高精度平衡，为实时、大规模物理建模提供了可行路径，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [212] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在LLM流水线中，由于上下文在各阶段间传递导致的指代消解偏差（deictic shift）问题。通过合成数据集测试不同模型在不同推理强度下的表现，发现部分模型如Gemini 3 Pro在低推理成本下表现优异，而GPT-5.2需高推理成本才能达到良好效果。错误决策多源于表面线索而非深层逻辑。建议在设计流程时明确指代内容以避免歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示和解决在Draft-verify-revise模式中因上下文传递导致的指代表达不一致问题，即‘deictic shift’现象，以提升多阶段大模型协作的准确性与可靠性。

Method: 采用合成数据集，包含10个基础示例，每个示例在三种条件下测试：由草稿阶段或验证阶段正确解析指代表达，以及元评估器所需独立推理量。使用e-values进行序列化测试，对六种来自三个供应商的模型在21种推理努力配置下进行主实验与消融实验（移除评分标签），并由另一大模型分析错误判决的理由。

Result: 平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的平均值）从0.156（低于随机水平）到接近完美不等。GPT-5.2从无推理时的0.156提升至最高推理强度下的0.942；而Gemini 3 Pro在所有推理强度下均保持高于0.94。在低推理成本下，Gemini 3 Pro的表现优于高推理成本下的GPT-5.2，且成本仅为后者的约5%。当元评估器出错时，其往往依赖表面线索而非操作性推理。

Conclusion: 在构建Draft-verify-revise流水线时，应警惕指代消解偏差，确保各阶段明确指代对象，避免因上下文传递引发误解，从而提高系统整体性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [213] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出会议动态预测基准（MDFB），基于2,207个真实会议和24,794个未来导向问题，评估模型在单次调用中生成多轮合理对话延续的能力。引入GLARE方法，通过对抗性模仿学习改进条件语言生成，利用判别器评分提供KL正则化策略奖励，使奖励空间随模型演进。GLARE在实用性（0.66）和人类自然度（0.70）上优于SFT和SPIN，但仍低于真实人类表现。MDFB还被用于比较通用模型的社会推理能力，支持基于参考的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以有效追踪会议中的议程、发言角色、参与者意图及分歧，尤其在长时多主体对话中。需要一个能评估对话延续合理性的基准与训练框架，以提升模型对复杂会议行为的理解与生成能力。

Method: 构建MDFB基准数据集，包含真实会议转录与未来问题；提出GLARE方法，基于对抗性模仿学习设计可演化奖励机制，通过判别器评估生成结果并优化策略；采用参考辅助判断评估通用模型在社交推理上的表现。

Result: GLARE在实用性与人类自然度上分别达到0.66和0.70的人类评价胜率，优于SFT与SPIN；MDFB成功作为社会推理评估平台，可用于对比闭源与开源模型的会议行为生成能力。

Conclusion: MDFB为会议对话延续任务提供了高质量评估基准，结合GLARE方法实现了更优的生成性能，同时展现出在通用模型评估方面的潜力，推动了对复杂社会交互建模的研究。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [214] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Soft-PNet 提出一种无需任务特定损失函数的神经符号模型，通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法在预计算的可行符号解缓存上进行软接地，利用原型分布指导概念定位，在少样本条件下实现与传统方法相当甚至更优的概念和标签预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号模型在训练时仅依赖最终标签，导致中间概念可能被错误推断（推理捷径问题）。虽然原型网络通过锚定概念到少数标注样本来缓解该问题，但其仍需为每项任务设计手工构造的可微分损失函数，限制了通用性。

Method: Soft-PNet 将概念接地重构为基于预计算缓存的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样过程，使用单个标注锚点构建的原型分布引导采样，并通过单一KL散度目标函数联合优化网络对概念的预测与缓存分布之间的匹配。该目标函数不依赖任务特定设计，适用于不可枚举的解空间。

Result: 在MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku和Kand-Logic等任务中，尽管无任何损失工程，Soft-PNet 在概念和标签层面表现与人工设计损失的原型网络相当，且能恢复其他软接地基线遗漏的概念，同时训练时间更低。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 实现了无需任务定制化损失函数的通用神经符号学习框架，有效避免推理捷径，提升概念可解释性与训练效率。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [215] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号框架用于自动约束获取（CA），通过引入神经Oracle Transformer模型来模拟用户响应并泛化概念知识，从而大幅减少对人类专家的依赖。该模型与FastCA引擎协同工作，将学习到的响应逐步优化为一致、可解释的约束网络，实现从数据中自动构建符号模型，无需先验领域知识。实验表明，该方法有效结合了数据驱动的模式识别与符号推理，为组合领域的模型自动化构建提供了稳健解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束获取方法通常需要大量与人类专家的交互，导致时间和查询成本高昂。因此，亟需一种能减少用户参与、实现自动化的约束获取方法。

Method: 提出一种神经符号框架，利用神经Oracle Transformer模型学习并泛化用户响应，结合FastCA引擎系统性地优化和生成一致、可解释的约束网络。

Result: 该框架成功实现了从数据中自动恢复结构化的符号模型，无需先验领域知识，且显著降低了用户参与度；实验验证了其在数据驱动模式识别与符号推理之间的有效融合。

Conclusion: 神经符号交互机制能够有效实现数据驱动与符号推理的协同，为自动化模型构建提供了一种高效、可靠的新范式。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [216] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多轮次原型网络（MEPN），通过聚合多个独立支持集的原型来降低原型方差，提升极低样本场景下的工业故障诊断性能。在DeFACTO数据集上，MEPN在单样本设置下表现显著优于传统原型网络，且在等量支持样本下与标准ProtoNet无统计差异，验证了原型累积是关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业故障诊断中，标注样本极少，传统原型网络因支持集过小导致类原型不稳定，亟需提升极低样本场景下的模型鲁棒性。

Method: 提出多轮次原型网络（MEPN），通过从多个不相交的支持集轮次中聚合原型，并取均值作为最终类别代表，无需修改编码器结构即可降低原型方差。

Result: 在DeFACTO数据集上，MEPN在每轮单样本设置（K=1）下达到	extbf{SensorOneShotGcpn\%}准确率，显著高于单轮基线；在相同10样本支持预算下，与K=10的ProtoNet无显著差异，表明原型累积是核心机制。

Conclusion: MEPN通过原型累积有效提升了极低样本场景下的故障诊断性能，证明了原型稳定性对少样本学习的关键作用，且无需改变模型架构即可实现显著改进。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [217] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种简化运动学模型（KM）和三种混合人工智能建模方法（HKM-NN），旨在通过腕部惯性测量单元（IMU）估计全身重心（COM）加速度。基于10名健康志愿者在六种步态活动和坐站转换中的数据，实验表明所提方法可行且有效，尤其HKM-NN模型显著优于传统KM模型，在噪声条件下表现出更强鲁棒性，凸显了生物力学结构与数据驱动学习结合在可穿戴传感中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部IMU虽广泛用于日常健康监测，但无法完整反映全身动态；身体重心（COM）是生理学上的参考标准，因此亟需从腕部IMU推断COM加速度，以提升可穿戴设备对全身运动的感知能力。

Method: 提出简化运动学模型（KM），并设计三种混合AI建模方法：基于人体运动学的神经网络（HKM-NN），包括串行学习（ser-）和两种并行学习方式（sim1-、sim2-）。模型基于腕部IMU数据训练与测试，融合先验生物力学知识与数据驱动学习。

Result: KM模型在步态活动中误差为6.7%–12.5%，坐站转换为5.6%；HKM-NN模型性能显著提升，步态活动误差降至5.3%–9.3%，坐站转换最佳误差达3.9%。sim1-/sim2-在高斯噪声下更鲁棒，而KM在椒盐噪声下表现更优。

Conclusion: 将生物力学结构与数据驱动学习相结合，能够有效提升从腕部IMU估计全身重心加速度的准确性与鲁棒性，适用于存在噪声的实际可穿戴传感场景。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [218] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL) is reevaluated in three roles: direct deletion signal, utility-preserving regularizer, and warm start for adversarial unlearning. After correcting implementation issues—image perturbations applied to raw images instead of normalized tensors, and label perturbations below float32 resolution—DPL fails to meet the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 across all seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent, and it underperforms simple warm-start baselines when computation time is considered. A limited check on Tiny ImageNet shows no advantage as a regularizer or warm start, though code preprocessing issues prevent firm conclusions. Results are limited to random instance deletion and do not rule out influence-based methods in other regimes. The authors release a matched evaluation protocol and audit checklist.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To rigorously evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL) in its three proposed roles—direct deletion, regularization, and warm start—by testing each independently under a consistent protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines, as prior evidence for weaker roles has been used to support stronger claims.

Method: The study conducts a role-matched evaluation of DPL using identical protocols and exact-seed retraining baselines. It audits the public implementation, identifies two correctness issues: image directions computed on augmented/normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and label perturbations falling below float32 precision. Corrections are made, and DPL is tested on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 across three seeds, plus a one-seed test on Tiny ImageNet.

Result: After corrections, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 across all three seeds. Utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds. When direction-computation time is included, DPL underperforms simple warm-start baselines. The Tiny ImageNet test shows no clear benefit as a regularizer or warm start, though code inconsistencies prevent definitive conclusions.

Conclusion: DPL does not reliably serve as a direct deletion signal, nor consistently act as a utility-preserving regularizer or effective warm start under the evaluated conditions. Implementation errors in prior work may have misled earlier findings. The study calls for standardized evaluation protocols and audit checks for future perturbation-based unlearning claims.

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [219] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory）的统一、隐私感知的记忆框架，支持多代理、多用户大语言模型系统持久化管理私有和共享记忆。AIM能动态区分信息为私有（仅限单个用户访问）或公共（全体可访问），并通过索引级访问控制保障数据安全，同时促进跨用户知识共享以提升响应质量。作者还构建了MUMBench（多用户记忆基准测试集），首个专用于评估多用户环境下记忆创建、检索、更新与删除等操作的公开数据集。在三次独立实验中，AIM在可见性分类上达到96.0%准确率，在严格操作和状态感知操作上分别达到58.8%和70.5%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型仅局限于单个用户会话，无法持续学习随时间演化的用户偏好；现有代理记忆系统多局限于个体用户层面，难以实现跨用户知识共享。为解决这一问题，需构建一个既能保护隐私又能促进协作的统一记忆系统。

Method: 提出AIM框架，通过动态分类机制将信息划分为私有或公共，并实施索引级访问控制，确保私有记忆仅对所有者可访问；同时设计并构建MUMBench数据集，用于评估多用户环境下的多种记忆操作性能。

Result: 在MUMBench数据集上的三轮测试中，AIM实现了96.0%的可见性分类准确率，58.8%的严格操作准确率，以及70.5%的状态感知操作准确率，验证了其在多用户场景下高效且安全地管理记忆的能力。

Conclusion: AIM框架成功实现了多用户、多代理系统中对私有与共享记忆的统一管理，在保障隐私的前提下有效促进了知识共享，显著提升了系统的协作能力与响应一致性。MUMBench作为首个相关基准数据集，为未来研究提供了重要评估工具。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [220] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Affective Agent 是一种三层次参考架构，用于在可穿戴设备上进行个性化干预推理，结合小型语言模型与生理证据、上下文和用户历史，在无云依赖和无需用户重训练的情况下做出何时、是否以及如何干预的决策。通过主机管理的结构化记忆演化实现个性化，而非更新模型权重。在室内环境质量控制场景中评估，结果显示记忆驱动的个性化和两阶段结构化推理提升了干预决策效果，展示了在可穿戴硬件上实现闭环个性化干预的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感计算虽推动了可穿戴设备状态推断的发展，但设备端关于何时、是否及如何干预的推理仍面临挑战，尤其在资源受限环境下缺乏自主决策能力。需要一种无需云端支持、无需为每个用户重新训练模型的解决方案，以实现个性化、自适应的实时干预。

Method: 提出 Affective Agent 三层次架构：感知层（处理生理信号）、个性化层（利用结构化记忆演化适应个体差异）、推理层（基于多源信息决策干预）。采用紧凑的子十亿参数语言模型，结合生理数据、上下文与用户历史，通过主机管理的记忆演化机制实现个性化，避免对模型权重进行用户级更新。

Result: 在模拟生成的纵向场景中评估，涵盖生理变化、上下文、信号质量与干预历史，结果表明记忆驱动的个性化和两阶段结构化推理显著提升了干预决策质量。系统可在本地完成决策，无需云端依赖，具备实际部署潜力。

Conclusion: Affective Agent 证明了在可穿戴设备级别实现闭环、个性化干预的可行性，通过本地推理与记忆演化机制，实现了高效、自适应的干预策略，为未来智能可穿戴系统提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [221] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoRA-RC 是一种新型的储层计算方法，通过低秩修正在线适应递归矩阵，利用流式预测误差驱动，保持系统稳定性与收敛性。该方法在固定基底上仅在线调整一个小的核心矩阵，并通过谱范数球投影和低通滤波确保递归矩阵始终处于有保证的收缩集合内，从而维持增量输入-状态稳定性。在具有突变参数漂移的洛伦兹系统上，相较于固定储层和仅读出适应，其预测误差分别降低56%和51%；消融实验表明，若移除投影机制，误差将增加40倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储层计算（RC）因使用静态储层，在系统发生漂移时性能下降；仅读出层适应不足以应对变化，而自由调整储层又可能破坏其回声态和增量稳定性特性，因此亟需一种既能自适应又可保证稳定性的新方法。

Method: 提出LoRA-RC，通过低秩修正方式在线调整递归矩阵，基于流式预测误差更新核心矩阵，结合谱范数球投影与低通滤波，确保每次更新后的递归矩阵仍位于认证的收缩集合中，从而保障系统的稳定性。

Result: 在洛伦兹系统中引入突变参数漂移后，LoRA-RC相比固定储层预测误差减少56%，相比仅读出适应减少51%；移除投影机制后误差增长超过40倍，验证了投影机制的关键作用。

Conclusion: LoRA-RC实现了在在线适应过程中对储层动态的稳定控制，既提升了对系统漂移的鲁棒性，又保持了储层计算原有的高效性和可靠性，为实际应用中的动态环境提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [222] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORE提出一种分离局部证据与持久人格状态更新的方法，通过不确定性感知的信念修正来选择性地更新用户偏好。PERSIST是一个用于评估长期交互中人格状态鲁棒性的基准测试。在ALOE、PersonaChat和PERSIST上，CORE提升了个性化对齐与鲁棒性，并在封闭槽位状态保真度上取得互补提升。人类评估和机制控制进一步验证了其在更新控制上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决个性化语言模型中人格漂移问题，即用户偏好随长时间互动而变化，需在真实变化时更新人格状态，同时避免因短暂、模糊或未明确信息导致的错误更新。

Method: CORE通过分离每轮对话中的局部证据与持久人格状态，采用不确定性感知的信念修正机制，仅在有充分依据时更新用户偏好；PERSIST作为后锚点基准，评估模型在序列交互压力下的鲁棒性，涵盖模糊性、冲突和受控社会影响等场景。

Result: CORE在ALOE、PersonaChat和PERSIST数据集上均表现出更好的个性化对齐与鲁棒性，尤其在封闭槽位状态保真度方面有显著提升；人类评估和机制控制显示其具备更优的更新控制能力，优于单纯增强生成或持久记忆。

Conclusion: CORE有效应对了人格漂移挑战，实现了对用户偏好变化的精准响应，同时保持对无关扰动的鲁棒性，为个性化语言模型提供了更可靠的人格状态管理机制。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [223] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BlueLM-GUI 是一个基于真实设备的35B-A3B移动GUI代理，通过三个核心原则解决工业部署中的三大挑战：确保每个样本都有价值、每次回放都是真实的、每个查询都能持续进化。该模型采用双轨管道与异构三系统共识评估，结合错误纠正与推导模块，最大化利用每条轨迹；通过在数百部真实手机上进行持续预训练、监督微调和智能体强化学习，确保训练与生产环境一致；设计了基于配额的多维基准测试方法，支持精准归因与系统性升级。最终在MobileGUI-VBench上达到87.4分（优于最佳闭源模型5.1分），在AndroidWorld上达84.9分，为开源模型最佳表现且媲美闭源模型，验证了真实设备驱动训练与迭代的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级移动GUI代理部署面临三大瓶颈：沙箱训练导致分布不匹配、真实设备失败数据未被充分利用、固定基准测试趋于饱和，无法有效指导模型迭代。

Method: 提出BlueLM-GUI，采用三阶段训练策略（持续预训练、监督微调、基于真实设备的强化学习）；构建双轨管道与异构三系统共识评估机制，结合错误纠正与推导模块以挖掘每条轨迹价值；设计基于配额的多维度基准测试体系，实现查询演化与能力精准追踪。

Result: 在MobileGUI-VBench上取得87.4分，超越最佳闭源模型5.1分；在AndroidWorld上达84.9分，为当前开源模型最优成绩，且接近闭源模型水平，证明模型具备强鲁棒性与可迁移性。

Conclusion: 将模型训练与迭代闭环建立在真实设备基础上，并贯彻‘每个样本都重要、每次回放都真实、每个查询都进化’三大原则，是实现高性能、高可靠移动GUI代理的关键路径。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [224] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文分析了三维高斯溅射（3DGS）中神经网络参数化趋势，从五个维度（属性解码、空间共享、视图条件解码、拓扑生成和摊销推理）进行系统评估，发现神经化并非单一模式，而是需根据任务选择性应用。实验表明，共享外观与不透明度可提升重建质量，但解码几何结构无额外增益。结论支持‘选择性神经化’策略：在保留显式点局部几何自由度的前提下，利用神经网络捕捉可复用相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法越来越多地引入神经网络来生成或共享高斯参数，但其设计选择缺乏系统性分析，导致对神经化作用的理解模糊。本文旨在厘清神经化在不同场景下的有效性边界，避免将复杂设计简化为二元判断。

Method: 提出五维分析框架，涵盖属性解码、空间共享、视图条件解码、拓扑生成和摊销推理；基于19个代表性方法进行对比分析，并在mip-NeRF 360数据集上开展受控实验，验证各组件对重建质量的影响。

Result: 共享外观与不透明度显著提升重建质量；解码几何结构未带来额外收益；神经化应有选择地应用于能捕捉可复用相关性的部分，而非全面替代显式表示。

Conclusion: 神经化不能被简化为一个统一标签。有效的参数化策略应是选择性地使用神经网络，在保持显式高斯局部自由度的同时，通过共享机制提升效率与质量。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [225] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了The Core算法，通过将代理分化到原始环境的不同子集，以实现更高效的自主控制演化。结果表明，针对特定子环境分化的代理在性能上优于全局分化或不同子环境分化的代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升代理在复杂环境中的适应能力，探索通过环境细分实现更高效、更具针对性的进化策略。

Method: 采用竞争共演化机制，利用局部交互、锦标赛选择、交叉和变异进行代理演化，并对环境进行细分以实现代理的差异化分组演化。

Result: 分化的代理在特定子环境中表现出更强的适应性和成功率，优于全局分化的代理以及在不同子环境中分化的代理。

Conclusion: 通过环境细分实现的代理分化显著提升了演化效率与任务表现，验证了该方法在复杂环境中的有效性。

Abstract: The Core is a unique competitive co-evolution algorithm that allows agents to evolve autonomous control without utilizing a traditional fitness function. The agents evolve via local interactions through tournament selection, crossover, and mutation, producing offspring by evolving better controllers. Previous works have shown The Core's ability to evolve agents capable of combat and navigation in the Xpilot video game. This research expands upon that premise by niching agents to specific subsets of the original environment The Core was tested in. Our results demonstrate the niched agents capacity for success over agents niched to the entire system and agents niched to different sub-environments.

</details>


### [226] [OneLA: Scaling Linear-Attention Decoding to Large Beams in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12399)
*Xiangrui Yang,Cheng Peng,Yunfeng Zhao,Liang Zeng,Ao Hu,Jiawei Yang,Shengzhe Wang,Jingshan Lv,Xiao Liang,Chen Yang,Jiaqiang Liu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OneLA 是一种针对生成推荐（GR）中大规模束搜索解码的线性注意力框架，通过共享提示状态和紧凑的可追加转换记录，显著降低内存占用和数据移动开销。它利用轻量级祖先索引追踪每个束的历史，并通过融合的GPU内核重用共享状态，实现1.54-2.46倍的端到端解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力服务系统在生成推荐任务中面临内存和通信开销大的问题，因为需要为每个束存储完整递归状态或重复回放共享历史。为解决这一挑战，提出更高效的状态表示与更新机制。

Method: OneLA 使用单一共享提示衍生状态表示所有束的状态，并以仅追加的方式记录各束的分歧转换；通过轻量级祖先索引管理历史路径，避免复制或移动数据；并采用融合的GPU内核复用共享状态，仅计算每步所需信息。

Result: OneLA 实现了1.54至2.46倍的端到端解码速度提升，同时大幅减少递归状态内存使用和数据传输量。

Conclusion: OneLA 有效解决了生成推荐中大规模束搜索带来的线性注意力扩展难题，通过共享状态与高效更新机制，在性能和资源消耗之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) relies on large-beam decoding to generate hundreds of candidate items, creating a new scaling challenge for recurrent linear attention. Existing linear attention serving systems either materialize a full recurrent state for every beam or repeatedly replay shared history, incurring substantial memory and traffic overhead. To address this, we present OneLA, a linear-attention decoding framework that exploits the shared prompt and short divergent suffixes of GR workloads. Specifically, OneLA represents all beam states using a single shared prompt-derived state and compact, append-only records of their divergent transitions. Using this representation, OneLA computes only the state information required at each decoding step, without reconstructing a full recurrent state for every beam. Furthermore, OneLA uses a lightweight ancestry index to track the transition records that make up each beam's history, allowing beams to be updated without moving or copying existing records. A fused GPU kernel further reuses the shared state across beams. Our analysis shows that OneLA achieves 1.54-2.46x end-to-end decode speedups while substantially reducing recurrent-state memory use and data movement.

</details>


### [227] [Decentralized Evolution of Hexapod Gaits with Independent Leg Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12400)
*Gary B. Parker,John Asaro,Jim O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种新型六足机器人行走方法，通过去中心化的进化算法独立演化每条腿的步态，在Webots仿真环境和Mantis六足机器人上实现无集中协调的个体腿控制器优化，从而涌现出高效、协调的行走行为。相比合作共进化方法，该方法在生成稳定自适应步态方面表现更优，并展现出有趣的协同现象。该策略降低了步态优化的复杂性，凸显了去中心化方法在可扩展、自适应机器人系统中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统六足机器人步态优化依赖集中式控制，导致系统复杂度高且难以扩展。为解决这一问题，本文旨在探索去中心化策略，使每条腿独立演化步态，以降低协调难度并提升系统的可扩展性与适应性。

Method: 采用去中心化的进化算法，独立演化每条腿的控制器参数；使用Webots仿真平台对Mantis六足机器人进行模拟实验；通过多轮进化评估步态性能，比较去中心化方法与合作共进化方法的效果。

Result: 所提出的去中心化方法在生成稳定、自适应步态方面优于合作共进化方法，表现出良好的涌现协调能力；同时显著降低了系统复杂性，验证了其在大规模机器人系统中应用的可行性。

Conclusion: 去中心化独立演化腿控制器是一种高效且可扩展的六足机器人步态优化策略，能够通过局部决策实现全局协调，为未来智能机器人系统的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to hexapod locomotion by evolving each leg's gait independently through a decentralized evolutionary algorithm. Using the Webots simulator and the Mantis hexapod robot, we optimize individual leg controllers without centralized coordination, allowing emergent behaviors to drive the development of efficient, coordinated locomotion. Our decentralized method is benchmarked against cooperative coevolution, demonstrating improved efficacy in generating stable and adaptive gaits while showing interesting emergent coordination. By enabling independent evolution of leg controllers, this method reduces the complexity of gait optimization and highlights the potential of decentralized strategies for scalable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond ID Embeddings: Process-Grounded Language Modeling for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12403)
*Minghang Liu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Qiang Qiu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为PLCD的流程感知语言认知诊断框架，利用语言衍生结构作为认知先验，并结合学生作答记录校准其认知状态。该方法通过大语言模型构建概念图谱与认知过程图，采用目标条件语义记忆检索相关历史作答，并通过基于过程的语言-认知映射器（含DA-MoE专家和过程级对比学习）将文本证据映射到统一认知空间。实验表明，PLCD在预测学生表现方面优于传统基线，且具备强大的认知迁移能力，实现了大语言模型计算能力与心理测量目标的融合，提升了冷启动场景下的鲁棒性与认知合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型依赖离散的ID嵌入表示学生、习题和概念，无法反映认知的连续性和语义关联，导致在面对新习题或概念时存在语义局限。为克服这一问题，本文旨在引入语言驱动的认知先验，使模型更贴近真实认知过程。

Method: 使用大语言模型构建概念图谱与认知过程图；通过目标条件语义记忆检索相关历史作答；设计过程接地的语言-认知映射器，结合DA-MoE专家与过程级对比学习，实现从文本证据到统一认知空间的映射。

Result: PLCD在预测学生表现上显著优于传统基线模型，并展现出优异的认知迁移能力，验证了语言先验与响应记录协同校准的有效性，增强了冷启动场景下的鲁棒性与认知合理性。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型生成的结构化语言先验与学生作答记录相结合，能够有效提升认知诊断模型的语义表达力与泛化能力，为个性化学习系统提供了更具认知合理性的技术路径。

Abstract: Cognitive Diagnosis Models (CDMs) play a pivotal role in personalized online learning. Traditional CDMs rely on discrete, ID-based embeddings to represent students, exercises, and concepts. This paradigm diverges from the nature of learner cognition, where knowledge is not stored and retrieved as isolated symbols. As a result, CDMs suffer from semantic limitations when new exercises or concepts appear. In this paper, we propose a Process-aware Language Cognitive Diagnosis (PLCD) framework that uses language-derived structures as cognitive priors and response records to calibrate student posterior states. PLCD leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct concept schemas and cognitive process graphs, and uses target-conditioned semantic memory to retrieve historical responses that are relevant to each target exercise. A process-grounded Language-to-Cognition Mapper with DA-MoE experts and process-level contrastive learning then maps the textual evidence into a unified cognitive space. Experimental results show that PLCD not only outperforms traditional baselines in predicting student performance but also exhibits strong cognitive transfer capabilities. These results connect the computational power of LLMs with the psychometric goal of measuring latent knowledge states, suggesting that structured language priors calibrated by response records can improve cold-start robustness and cognitive grounding.

</details>


### [229] [VRL-Bench: Benchmarking agents on computer control tasks under finite trial budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12404)
*Yu Bai,Yukai Miao,Dawei Wang,Li Chen,Yanyu Ren,Yuqian Shi,Dan Li,Ying Xiong,Chengqiu Tan,Run Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VRL-Bench用于公平评估有限试错次数下的语言代理学习效果。实验表明，现有基于反思的文本记忆方法在某些场景提升成功率，但在其他场景反而降低。通过回放实验发现，使用反思可能减少成功概率，揭示了利用经验与持续探索之间的权衡。为此，提出VEX$^2$，一种由语言模型联合调度策略与剩余试错预算的探索-利用机制，在所有六个测试场景中均优于单纯重试策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有试错学习方法在不同任务中表现不一，缺乏统一评估框架；同时，反思机制可能抑制探索，导致性能下降。需要一种能平衡探索与利用、并可公平评估的方法。

Method: 构建VRL-Bench评估基准，对比多种反思型记忆方法；设计VEX$^2$，利用语言模型动态决策策略选择与试错预算分配，实现探索与利用的协同优化。

Result: VEX$^2$是唯一在所有六种设置中均显著提升成功率达到超过单纯重试的方法；其他方法在部分场景有效，但存在退化现象。

Conclusion: 语言代理的试错学习需兼顾探索与利用，仅依赖反思会带来负面影响。VEX$^2$通过智能调度实现了更优的整体性能，为未来复杂任务中的语言代理学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning from trial and error is a promising way to improve language agents on complex tasks such as computer control. Reflexion introduced verbal reinforcement learning, which turns failed trials into text that guides later attempts without updating model parameters. We introduce VRL-Bench, a harness for fair evaluation of trial-and-error learning under finite trial budgets. Across three models on MiniWoB and WebShop, we evaluate updates from several prominent verbal-memory methods spanning Reflexion and later work: each improves observed success over memory-free retry in some settings but reduces it in others. Replay experiments show that using reflection can reduce success rates, revealing a trade-off between exploiting experience and continued exploration. We propose VEX$^2$, a verbal exploration--exploitation scheduler that uses a language model to jointly select policies and allocate the remaining trial budget. VEX$^2$ is the only evaluated update to achieve positive observed success-rate gains over retry in all six settings.

</details>


### [230] [SoK: Rethinking Jailbreaking in the Era of Agentic AI: Attacks, Defenses, and Practical Consideration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12413)
*Md Jueal Mia,Yanzhao Wu,Selcuk Uluagac,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化知识（SoK）研究，重新审视现代大语言模型（LLMs）和代理型AI系统中的越狱安全问题。作者构建了涵盖用户交互、规划推理、记忆、工具使用及代理间通信的攻击与防御统一分类体系，并提出一个融合安全性、实用性与效率的评估框架。实证研究表明：强原生对齐不等于对抗性越狱鲁棒；防御效果高度依赖模型、攻击方式和组件，且常伴随过度拒绝、性能下降与延迟增加；最终响应成功过滤可能掩盖中间环节（如规划、记忆、工具调用）的严重安全隐患。因此，需从以响应为中心转向跨层、执行感知的安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型向具备推理、规划、工具调用等能力的代理型AI演进，其原生安全对齐显著增强，但现有越狱攻击与防御研究多基于早期模型，无法有效反映当前场景。因此亟需重新评估经典结论在现代语境下的有效性，明确哪些安全机制仍适用，哪些需要革新。

Method: 采用系统化知识（SoK）方法，重构越狱安全的全代理执行流程视角；设计统一攻击与防御分类体系，覆盖用户交互、规划推理、记忆、工具使用、代理通信等关键环节；提出安全性-实用性-效率三维度评估框架；在统一代理框架下进行受控实验，对比代表性攻击与防御策略的表现。

Result: 1. 强原生对齐不能保证对抗性越狱的鲁棒性；2. 防御效果具有高度模型、攻击和组件依赖性，常导致过量拒绝、功能损失与延迟上升；3. 低最终响应成功率可能掩盖中间阶段（如计划、记忆、工具交互）存在的严重安全隐患。

Conclusion: 越狱安全研究应从以最终输出为中心转向跨层、执行感知的防护范式，重点保护代理状态、组件间转换过程及外部行为，同时兼顾实际可用性与运行效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving from conversational assistants into agentic AI systems that reason, plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, communicate with other agents, and execute multi-step tasks. At the same time, modern models exhibit substantially stronger native safety alignment than earlier generations on which many jailbreak attacks and defenses were originally studied. This shift raises a fundamental question: \textit{which established jailbreak-security findings remain valid in the era of modern LLMs and agentic AI?} We address this question through a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) that reframes jailbreak security around the full agentic execution pipeline. We develop unified taxonomies of attacks and defenses spanning user interaction, planning and reasoning, memory, tool use, and inter-agent communication, and introduce a security--utility--efficiency evaluation framework that separates native harmful-prompt safety, adversarial jailbreak robustness, and agent-level security outcomes. We further conduct a controlled empirical study of representative attacks and defenses within a common agentic framework. Our results reveal three important gaps. First, strong native alignment does not imply robustness to adversarial jailbreaks. Second, defense effectiveness is highly model-, attack-, and component-dependent and can come at substantial cost in over-refusal, utility, and latency. Third, low final-response attack success can mask severe intermediate compromise: planning, memory, and tool interactions may remain unsafe even when the final response is successfully filtered. These findings motivate a shift from response-centric jailbreak defense toward cross-layer, execution-aware security that protects agent state, component transitions, and external actions while preserving practical utility and efficiency.

</details>


### [231] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HORIZON 是一个在 Lux AI Season 3 中表现优异的分层智能体，结合了对称感知空间感知、双记忆信念追踪、以遗迹为中心的图注意力、信息增益驱动探索以及对手条件策略混合。该智能体通过分离短期控制与跨比赛元推理，辅以辅助信念和世界模型目标来稳定学习，在大规模 JAX 模拟器中使用 PPO 训练，能够显式推断隐藏游戏参数和对手风格。实验表明其在胜率、适应性及联赛评分方面均优于强大多层和前馈基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测、随机化比赛动态和五局三胜制的复杂环境中，传统智能体难以同时实现战术执行与快速适应。因此需要一种能有效处理不确定性、动态变化并具备长期战略规划能力的新型智能体架构。

Method: 采用分层结构设计，包含对称感知的空间感知模块、双记忆信念追踪机制、以遗迹为中心的图注意力网络、基于信息增益的探索策略，以及根据对手风格调整的策略混合方法。通过辅助的信念和世界模型任务提升训练稳定性，并使用 PPO 算法在大规模 JAX 模拟器中进行训练。

Result: HORIZON 在匹配胜率、回合胜率、适应性提升和联赛评分上均显著优于现有递归和前馈基线模型，展现出强大的战术执行力与快速适应能力。

Conclusion: HORIZON 成功实现了在复杂多变环境下的高效决策，其分层设计与多模块协同机制为高阶博弈智能体提供了可扩展且稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Lux AI Season 3 requires agents to act under partial observability, randomized episode level dynamics, and a best of five match structure that rewards both tactical execution and fast adaptation. We present HORIZON, a hierarchical agent that combines symmetry aware spatial perception, dual memory belief tracking, relic centric graph attention, information gain driven exploration, and an opponent conditioned policy mixture. HORIZON separates short horizon control from cross match meta reasoning, while auxiliary belief and world model objectives stabilize learning. Trained with PPO in a large scale JAX simulator, the resulting agent explicitly infers hidden game parameters and opponent style.
